              "archived",
              "item_id",
              "project_id",
              "status_values"
            ],
            "properties": {
              "archived": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "item_id": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 256,
                "type": "string"
              },
              "project_id": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 256,
                "type": "string"
              },
              "status_values": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "type": "object",
                  "required": [
                    "field_id",
                    "option_id",
                    "name"
                  ],
                  "properties": {
                    "field_id": {
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 256,
                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    "option_id": {
                      "anyOf": [
                        {
                          "minLength": 1,
                          "maxLength": 256,
                          "type": "string"
                        },
                        {
                          "type": "null"
                        }
                      ]
                    },
                    "name": {
                      "anyOf": [
                        {
                          "minLength": 1,
                          "maxLength": 1024,
                          "type": "string"
                        },
                        {
                          "type": "null"
                        }
                      ]
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "ConfigurationOptions": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "projects",
        "status_fields",
        "status_options",
        "profiles",
        "completeness",
        "diagnostics",
        "retry_at_ms"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "projects": {
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "type": "object",
          "required": [
            "items",
            "next_cursor",
            "has_more",
            "event_cursor"
          ],
          "properties": {
            "items": {
              "maxItems": 100,
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "$ref": "#/$defs/GitHubProject"
              }
            },
            "next_cursor": {
              "anyOf": [
                {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 512,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              ]
            },
            "has_more": {
              "type": "boolean"
            },
            "event_cursor": {
              "pattern": "^[0-9]+$",
              "maxLength": 20,
              "type": "string"
            }
          }
        },
        "status_fields": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/StatusField"
          }
        },
        "status_options": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "type": "object",
            "required": [
              "id",
              "name"
            ],
            "properties": {
              "id": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 256,
                "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                "type": "string"
              },
              "name": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 1024,
                "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                "type": "string"
              }
            }
          }
        },
        "profiles": {
          "maxItems": 100,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/StoredExecutionProfile"
          }
        },
        "completeness": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "complete",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "partial",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "unavailable",
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "diagnostics": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/GitHubDiagnostic"
          }
        },
        "retry_at_ms": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "minimum": 0,
              "maximum": 9007199254740991,
              "type": "integer"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        }
      }
    },
    "ConfigurationOptionsQuery": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "owner_id": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 256,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "owner_kind": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "user",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "organization",
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "github_project_id": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 256,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "status_field_id": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 256,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "cursor": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 512,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "limit": {
          "pattern": "^(?:[1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|100)$",
          "type": "string"
        }
      }
    },

      "anyOf": [
        {
          "additionalProperties": false,

          ],

              "const": "configured",
              "type": "string"
            },
            "value": {
              "minimum": 0,
              "type": "number"
            },
            "unit": {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 64,
              "type": "string"
            }
          }
        },
        {
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "type": "object",
          "required": [
            "state"
          ],
          "properties": {
            "state": {
              "const": "not_configured",
              "type": "string"
            }
          }
        }
      ]
    },
    "CreationBoardOperationKind": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "const": "create_board",
          "type": "string"

        {

          "type": "string"
        },
        {

          "type": "string"
        },
        {

        },
        {

        },

        },
        {
          "const": "failed_known",
          "type": "string"
        },
        {

        }
      ]

    "CriteriaPreview": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "executable",
        "objective",
        "criteria",
        "digest",
        "reasons"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "executable": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "objective": {
          "maxLength": 1000000,
          "type": "string"
        },
        "criteria": {
          "maxItems": 1000,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/Criterion"
          }
        },
        "digest": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "reasons": {
          "maxItems": 20,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 128,
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "Criterion": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "id",
        "text",
        "occurrence_index",
        "explicit"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "id": {
          "pattern": "^ac-[1-9][0-9]*$",
          "maxLength": 128,
          "type": "string"
        },
        "text": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 65536,
          "type": "string"
        },
        "occurrence_index": {
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 9007199254740991,
          "type": "integer"
        },
        "explicit": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      }
    },
    "DeletionBlocker": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "reason",
        "recovery"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "reason": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 2048,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "recovery": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 2048,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        }
      }
    },
    "DeletionCompleted": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "operation_id",
        "state"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "operation_id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "state": {
          "const": "succeeded",
          "type": "string"
        }
      }
    },
    "DeletionConfirmation": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "confirmed",
        "authorization_digest"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "confirmed": {
          "const": true,
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "authorization_digest": {
          "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$",
          "minLength": 64,
          "maxLength": 64,
          "type": "string"
        }
      }
    },
    "DeletionPending": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "operation_id",
        "project_id",
        "project_name",
        "state",
        "revision",
        "targets",
        "blockers",
        "can_retry"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "operation_id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "project_id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "project_name": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 256,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "state": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "accepted",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "running",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "pending",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "reconfirmation_required",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "finalizing",
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "revision": {
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 9007199254740991,
          "type": "integer"
        },
        "targets": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/DeletionTarget"
          }
        },
        "blockers": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/DeletionBlocker"
          }
        },
        "can_retry": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      }
    },

        "authorization_digest",
        "source_kind",
        "targets",
        "blockers",

          "type": "string"
        },

          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 256,

        },
        "project_revision": {
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 9007199254740991,
          "type": "integer"
        },

          "maxLength": 64,

        },
        "source_kind": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "local",

            },

            }
          ]
        },
        "targets": {

          }

          }
        },

        }

            {

            },

          ]

              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "recovery": {

            }

        },

          ]
        },
        "path": {
          "minLength": 1,

        },
        "state": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "pending",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "removing",

            },
            {
              "const": "removed",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "blocked",

            }

        },

        }

          "type": "string"

      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [

        "authorized_mode",
        "review_state",
        "outcome",

        "approved_ref",
        "code_location",

        "pull_request_url",
        "checkout_updated",
        "pending_effects",
        "limitations",
        "criteria_satisfied",
        "criteria_total",
        "resolved_findings",
        "details_url"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "attempt_id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "delivery_id": {

          "type": "string"
        },

        },

          "anyOf": [
            {
              "anyOf": [

                  "const": "pending",
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "const": "approved",
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "const": "rejected",
                  "type": "string"
                }
              ]
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "outcome": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "pending",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "blocked",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "const": "icr_approved",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {

          "required": [
            "format_version",
            "owner_id",
            "board_name",
            "marker",
            "repository_id",
            "project_id",
            "status_field_id",
            "expected_status_names",
            "view_id",
            "view_name",
            "view_layout"
          ],
          "properties": {
            "format_version": {
              "const": 1,
              "type": "number"
            },
            "owner_id": {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 256,
              "type": "string"
            },
            "board_name": {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 256,
              "type": "string"
            },
            "marker": {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 512,
              "type": "string"
            },
            "repository_id": {
              "anyOf": [
                {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              ]
            },
            "project_id": {
              "anyOf": [
                {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              ]
            },
            "status_field_id": {
              "anyOf": [

                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              ]
            },
            "expected_status_names": {
              "minItems": 5,
              "maxItems": 5,
              "type": "array",

                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 128,
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "view_id": {
              "anyOf": [
                {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              ]
            },
            "view_name": {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 256,
              "type": "string"
            },
            "view_layout": {
              "const": "BOARD_LAYOUT",
              "type": "string"
            }
          }
        },
        "receipt": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "additionalProperties": false,
              "type": "object",
              "required": [
                "format_version",
                "observed_at_ms"
              ],
              "properties": {
                "format_version": {
                  "const": 1,
                  "type": "number"
                },
                "observed_at_ms": {
                  "minimum": 0,
                  "maximum": 9007199254740991,
                  "type": "integer"
                },
                "project_id": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "owner_id": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "title": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "project_url": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 2048,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "repository_ids": {
                  "maxItems": 100,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "minLength": 1,
                    "maxLength": 256,
                    "type": "string"
                  }
                },
                "status_field_id": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "status_field_name": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "status_options": {
                  "maxItems": 20,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "additionalProperties": false,
                    "type": "object",
                    "required": [
                      "id",
                      "name"
                    ],
                    "properties": {
                      "id": {
                        "minLength": 1,
                        "maxLength": 256,
                        "type": "string"
                      },
                      "name": {
                        "minLength": 1,
                        "maxLength": 128,
                        "type": "string"
                      }
                    }
                  }
                },
                "view_id": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "view_name": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 256,
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "view_layout": {
                  "const": "BOARD_LAYOUT",
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "group_by_field_ids": {
                  "maxItems": 20,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "minLength": 1,
                    "maxLength": 256,
                    "type": "string"
                  }
                },
                "visible_field_ids": {
                  "maxItems": 100,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "minLength": 1,
                    "maxLength": 256,
                    "type": "string"
                  }
                }
              }
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "reason": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 128,
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "created_at_ms": {
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 9007199254740991,
          "type": "integer"
        },
        "sent_at_ms": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "minimum": 0,
              "maximum": 9007199254740991,
              "type": "integer"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "confirmed_at_ms": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "minimum": 0,
              "maximum": 9007199254740991,
              "type": "integer"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        }
      }
    },

        "sent_at_ms": {

              "type": "integer"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "confirmed_at_ms": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]

    "HttpCriterionDetail": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "id",
        "text",
        "occurrence_index",
        "explicit",
        "producer",
        "reviewer"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "id": {
          "pattern": "^ac-[1-9][0-9]*$",
          "maxLength": 128,
          "type": "string"
        },
        "text": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 65536,
          "type": "string"
        },
        "occurrence_index": {
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 9007199254740991,
          "type": "integer"
        },
        "explicit": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "producer": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "additionalProperties": false,
              "type": "object",
              "required": [
                "verdict",
                "evidence"

                "verdict": {
                  "anyOf": [
                    {
                      "const": "passed",

                    {

                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    {
                      "const": "not_verified",
                      "type": "string"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "evidence": {
                  "minItems": 1,
                  "maxItems": 20,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "minLength": 1,
                    "maxLength": 4096,
                    "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                    "type": "string"

            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "reviewer": {

              "additionalProperties": false,
              "type": "object",
              "required": [
                "verdict",
                "evidence"
              ],
              "properties": {

                  "anyOf": [
                    {
                      "const": "passed",
                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    {
                      "const": "failed",
                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    {
                      "const": "not_verified",
                      "type": "string"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "evidence": {
                  "minItems": 1,
                  "maxItems": 20,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "minLength": 1,
                    "maxLength": 4096,
                    "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                    "type": "string"
                  }
                }
              }
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        }
      }
    },

      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [

        "capture_state",
        "git_ref",
        "repository_relative",
        "content_identity",
        "commit_sha",
        "has_delta",
        "diagnostic",

        "warnings"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "attempt_id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "producer_passage_id": {
          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "round": {
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 6,

        "base_sha": {
          "pattern": "^(?:[a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})$",
          "type": "string"

        "capture_state": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "const": "pending",

            {

            },
            {
              "const": "captured",

            {
              "const": "failed",
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "git_ref": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "null"
            },
            {
              "pattern": "^refs/factory/deliveries/[a-f0-9-]{36}$",
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "repository_relative": {
          "pattern": "^git/[a-f0-9-]{36}\\.git$",
          "type": "string"

        "content_identity": {
          "anyOf": [

              "type": "null"
            },
            {

              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "commit_sha": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "null"
            },
            {
              "pattern": "^(?:[a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})$",
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "has_delta": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "null"
            },
            {
              "type": "boolean"
            }
          ]
        },
        "diagnostic": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "null"
            },
            {
              "maxLength": 128,
              "type": "string"
            }
          ]
        },
        "captured_at_ms": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "null"
            },
            {
              "minimum": 0,
              "type": "integer"
            }
          ]
        },
        "revision": {
          "minimum": 1,
          "type": "integer"
        },
        "warnings": {
          "maxItems": 1000,

                {

                },
                {
                  "const": "rejected",

            "content_identity": {
              "anyOf": [
                {
                  "type": "null"
                },
                {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              ]

        },
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/HttpPassageSummary"

        {

            },
            {

        },

          "minimum": 0,

            {
              "minimum": 0,
              "maximum": 9007199254740991,

            },
            {

            }

        },

    "HttpImplementerResult": {
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "format_version",
        "summary",
        "writes_stopped",
        "criteria",
        "checks",
        "additional_changes_justification"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "format_version": {
          "const": 1,
          "type": "number"
        },
        "summary": {
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 8192,
          "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "writes_stopped": {
          "const": true,
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "declared_files": {
          "maxItems": 1000,
          "uniqueItems": true,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 4096,
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "finding_responses": {
          "maxItems": 1000,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "type": "object",
            "required": [
              "finding_id",
              "response_type",
              "response",
              "evidence"
            ],
            "properties": {
              "finding_id": {
                "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$",
                "type": "string"
              },
              "response_type": {
                "anyOf": [
                  {
                    "const": "corrected",
                    "type": "string"
                  },
                  {
                    "const": "disputed",
                    "type": "string"
                  },
                  {
                    "type": "null"
                  }
                ]
              },
              "response": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 4096,
                "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                "type": "string"
              },
              "evidence": {
                "minItems": 1,
                "maxItems": 20,
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 4096,
                  "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        },
        "criteria": {
          "minItems": 1,
          "maxItems": 1000,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/HttpCriterionOutcome"
          }
        },
        "checks": {
          "maxItems": 50,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "type": "object",
            "required": [
              "id",
              "outcome",
              "evidence"
            ],
            "properties": {
              "id": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 128,
                "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                "type": "string"
              },
              "outcome": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 1024,
                "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                "type": "string"
              },
              "evidence": {
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 4096,
                "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
                "type": "string"
              }
            }
          }
        },
        "additional_changes_justification": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 8192,
              "pattern": ".*\\S.*",
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        }
      }
    },

              "issue_id",

                },
                {

                }

                },
                {

                }
              ]
            },

              "const": false,

          }
        },

              "field": {
